Deprecated options
******************

* drivers/bluetooth

  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_BT_HCI_SETUP`: Enable the HCI vendor-specific Setup function [DEPRECATED].

* drivers/clock_control

  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_CLOCK_CONTROL_NRF`: Enable support for the Nordic Semiconductor nRFxx series SoC clock

* drivers/dma

  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_DMA_XILINX_AXI_DMA_DISABLE_CACHE_WHEN_ACCESSING_SG_DESCRIPTORS`: Deprecated. This option has been renamed to

* drivers/sensor

  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_MCUX_LPCMP_TRIGGER`: Enable trigger support for the NXP LPCMP.

* drivers/spi

  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_SPI_SLAVE`: Deprecated alias for SPI_PERIPHERAL. Use SPI_PERIPHERAL

* Kconfig (top level)

  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_DEPRECATION_TEST`: This option is selected by tests which check functionality of deprecated features. It
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_FLASH_CODE_PARTITION_USING_FIXED_PARTITIONS`: If this item is selected then it is because your device is using ``fixed-partitions`` or
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_LEGACY_GENERATED_INCLUDE_PATH`: Allow applications and libraries to use the Zephyr legacy include

* lib

  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_CPU_LOAD_METRIC`: Deprecated. Use CPU_LOAD together with the
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_RING_BUFFER`: This option is deprecated and slated for removal. It switches the ring

* modules

  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_MCUBOOT_BOOTUTIL_LIB_FOR_DIRECT_XIP`: Adds support for setting for test and confirming images
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_NRFX_CLOCK_LF_CAL_ENABLED`
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_NRFX_CLOCK_LF_SRC_FULL_SWING`
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_NRFX_CLOCK_LF_SRC_LOW_SWING`
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_NRFX_CLOCK_LF_SRC_RC`
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_NRFX_CLOCK_LF_SRC_SYNTH`
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_NRFX_CLOCK_LF_SRC_XTAL`
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_NRFX_CLOCK_LFXO_TWO_STAGE_ENABLED`
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_TFM_ZEPHYR_4_0_TO_4_2_COMPATIBILITY`: This option is deprecated, please use TFM_ZEPHYR_4_2_COMPATIBILITY instead.
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_WIFI_NM_WPA_SUPPLICANT_WEP`: Deprecated: WEP is legacy broken crypto and will be removed.

* soc/nordic

  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_NRF_PLATFORM_HALTIUM`: This option has been removed. Use SOC_SERIES_NRF54H or SOC_SERIES_NRF92
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_NRF_PLATFORM_LUMOS`: This option has been removed. Use SOC_SERIES_NRF54L or SOC_SERIES_NRF71

* soc/raspberrypi

  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_SOC_RP2350A_HAZARD3`: Use the RP2350A with a Hazard3 core in both 'sockets'.
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_SOC_RP2350A_M33`: Use the RP2350A with a Cortex-M33 core in both 'sockets'.
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_SOC_RP2350B_HAZARD3`: Use the RP2350B with a Hazard3 core in both 'sockets'.
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_SOC_RP2350B_M33`: Use the RP2350B with a Cortex-M33 core in both 'sockets'.

* subsys/bluetooth

  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_BT_CUSTOM`: Select a custom, non-HCI based stack. Deprecated: the option dates

* subsys/debug

  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_ASSERT_NO_COND_INFO`: This option removes the assert condition from the printed assert
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_ASSERT_NO_FILE_INFO`: This option removes the name and the path of the source file
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_ASSERT_NO_MSG_INFO`: This option removes the additional message from the printed assert.
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_ASSERT_VERBOSE`: This option enables printing an assert message with information about
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_FORCE_NO_ASSERT`: This boolean option disables Zephyr assertion testing even

* subsys/net

  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_LLMNR_RESOLVER`: This option enables link local multicast name resolution client side
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_LLMNR_RESPONDER`: This option enables the LLMNR responder support for Zephyr.
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_NET_L2_PTP`: This Kconfig is deprecated. Use NET_L2_PTP_TIMESTAMPING instead.
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_NET_MCAST_ROUTE_MLD_REPORTS`: Determines whether a multicast route entry should be advertised
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_NET_ROUTE_MCAST`: Activates multicast routing/forwarding.
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_NET_ROUTING`: This symbol is deprecated, use NET_IPV6_FORWARDING instead.

Changed options
***************

Options whose type or default values changed.

* arch

  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_ARCH_SUPPORTS_ROM_OFFSET`: defaults ``y if ARM || X86 || ARM64 || RISCV || ARC`` -> none
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_MAX_THREAD_BYTES`: defaults ``2 if USERSPACE`` -> ``5 if MP_MAX_NUM_CPUS > 4 && USERSPACE``, ``4 if MP_MAX_NUM_CPUS > 2 && USERSPACE``, ``3 if MP_MAX_NUM_CPUS > 1 && USERSPACE``, ``2 if USERSPACE``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_MAX_XLAT_TABLES`: defaults ``32 if USERSPACE && TEST && SMP && ARM_MMU && (CPU_CORTEX_A || CPU_AARCH64_CORTEX_R) && ARM64``, ``24 if USERSPACE && TEST && ARM_MMU && (CPU_CORTEX_A || CPU_AARCH64_CORTEX_R) && ARM64``, ``20 if USERSPACE && ARM64_VA_BITS >= 40 && ARM_MMU && (CPU_CORTEX_A || CPU_AARCH64_CORTEX_R) && ARM64``, ``16 if USERSPACE && ARM_MMU && (CPU_CORTEX_A || CPU_AARCH64_CORTEX_R) && ARM64``, ``12 if ARM64_VA_BITS >= 40 && ARM_MMU && (CPU_CORTEX_A || CPU_AARCH64_CORTEX_R) && ARM64``, ``8 if ARM_MMU && (CPU_CORTEX_A || CPU_AARCH64_CORTEX_R) && ARM64`` -> ``32 if USERSPACE && TEST && SMP && ARM_MMU && (CPU_CORTEX_A || CPU_AARCH64_CORTEX_R) && ARM64``, ``28 if USERSPACE && TEST && ARM_MMU && (CPU_CORTEX_A || CPU_AARCH64_CORTEX_R) && ARM64``, ``20 if USERSPACE && ARM64_VA_BITS >= 40 && ARM_MMU && (CPU_CORTEX_A || CPU_AARCH64_CORTEX_R) && ARM64``, ``16 if USERSPACE && ARM_MMU && (CPU_CORTEX_A || CPU_AARCH64_CORTEX_R) && ARM64``, ``16 if SMP && ARM64_VA_BITS >= 36 && ARM_MMU && (CPU_CORTEX_A || CPU_AARCH64_CORTEX_R) && ARM64``, ``12 if ARM64_VA_BITS >= 40 && ARM_MMU && (CPU_CORTEX_A || CPU_AARCH64_CORTEX_R) && ARM64``, ``8 if ARM_MMU && (CPU_CORTEX_A || CPU_AARCH64_CORTEX_R) && ARM64``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_XTENSA_ENABLE_BACKTRACE`: defaults ``y if (SOC_SERIES_ESP32 || SOC_FAMILY_INTEL_ADSP || SOC_XTENSA_DC233C || SOC_MIMXRT595S_F1) && XTENSA`` -> none
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_XTENSA_MMU_PTEVADDR_SHIFT`: defaults ``29 if XTENSA_MMU_PTEVADDR_20000000 && XTENSA_MMU && CPU_HAS_MMU && XTENSA`` -> ``29 if XTENSA_MMU_PTEVADDR_20000000 && XTENSA_MMU && CPU_HAS_MMU && XTENSA``, ``29 if XTENSA_MMU_PTEVADDR_60000000 && XTENSA_MMU && CPU_HAS_MMU && XTENSA``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_XTENSA_MMU_PTEVADDR`: defaults ``0x20000000 if XTENSA_MMU_PTEVADDR_20000000 && XTENSA_MMU && CPU_HAS_MMU && XTENSA`` -> ``0x20000000 if XTENSA_MMU_PTEVADDR_20000000 && XTENSA_MMU && CPU_HAS_MMU && XTENSA``, ``0x60000000 if XTENSA_MMU_PTEVADDR_60000000 && XTENSA_MMU && CPU_HAS_MMU && XTENSA``

* boards/shields

  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_BT_RX_STACK_SIZE`: defaults ``4096 if LIBLC3 && BT && (BT_NXP_IW416 || BT_NXP_NW612 || BT_NXP_IW610 || NXP_IW416 || NXP_IW61X || NXP_IW610)`` -> ``4096 if LIBLC3 && BT && (BT_NXP_IW416 || BT_NXP_NW612 || BT_NXP_IW610 || NXP_IW416 || NXP_IW61X || NXP_IW610)``, ``2800 if (BT_SETTINGS || BT_SMP) && !BT_MESH_GATT_CLIENT && BT && (BT_NXP_IW416 || BT_NXP_NW612 || BT_NXP_IW610 || NXP_IW416 || NXP_IW61X || NXP_IW610)``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_BT`: defaults none -> ``y if SHIELD_NXP_M2_2EL_2LL_IEEE802154``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_GNSS`: defaults none -> ``y if SHIELD_X_NUCLEO_GNSS1A1``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_GPIO`: defaults none -> ``y if SHIELD_NXP_MX8_DSI_OLED1A``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_HDLC_RCP_IF_SPI_ALIGN_ALLOWANCE`: defaults ``0 if HDLC_RCP_IF_SPI && HDLC_RCP_IF`` -> ``16 if SHIELD_NXP_M2_2EL_2LL_IEEE802154``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_HDLC_RCP_IF_SPI_SMALL_PACKET_SIZE`: defaults ``32 if HDLC_RCP_IF_SPI && HDLC_RCP_IF`` -> ``48 if SHIELD_NXP_M2_2EL_2LL_IEEE802154``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_I2C`: defaults none -> ``y if SHIELD_NXP_M2_2EL_2LL_IEEE802154``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_LV_Z_AREA_X_ALIGNMENT_WIDTH`: defaults ``1 if LVGL`` -> ``2 if LVGL && SHIELD_ZC143AC72MIPI``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_LV_Z_AREA_Y_ALIGNMENT_WIDTH`: defaults ``1 if LVGL`` -> ``2 if LVGL && SHIELD_ZC143AC72MIPI``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_NET_IF_MAX_IPV4_COUNT`: defaults ``NET_VLAN_COUNT if NET_VLAN && NET_VLAN_COUNT > 0 && NET_IPV4 && !NET_RAW_MODE && NETWORKING``, ``2 if NRF70_ENABLE_DUAL_VIF && NET_IPV4 && !NET_RAW_MODE && NETWORKING``, ``2 if NET_LOOPBACK && NET_IPV4 && !NET_RAW_MODE && NETWORKING``, ``1 if NET_IPV4 && !NET_RAW_MODE && NETWORKING`` -> ``3 if ETH_ADIN2111 && SHIELD_AD_APARDPFW_SL``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_NET_IF_MAX_IPV6_COUNT`: defaults ``NET_VLAN_COUNT if NET_VLAN && NET_VLAN_COUNT > 0 && NET_IPV6 && !NET_RAW_MODE && NETWORKING``, ``2 if NRF70_ENABLE_DUAL_VIF && NET_IPV6 && !NET_RAW_MODE && NETWORKING``, ``2 if NET_LOOPBACK && NET_IPV6 && !NET_RAW_MODE && NETWORKING``, ``1 if NET_IPV6 && !NET_RAW_MODE && NETWORKING`` -> ``3 if ETH_ADIN2111 && SHIELD_AD_APARDPFW_SL``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_NET_L2_OPENTHREAD`: defaults none -> ``y if SHIELD_NXP_M2_2EL_2LL_IEEE802154``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_SENSOR`: defaults none -> ``y if FUEL_GAUGE && SHIELD_RAKWIRELESS_RAK19007``

* drivers/adc

  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_LPADC_DO_OFFSET_CALIBRATION`: defaults none -> ``y if ADC_MCUX_LPADC && ADC``

* drivers/bluetooth

  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_BT_BUF_EVT_DISCARDABLE_SIZE`: defaults ``84 if BT_NXP && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS`` -> ``255 if BT_HCI_ESP_HOSTED_MCU && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_BT_EXT_ADV_LEGACY_SUPPORT`: defaults none -> ``y if BT_EXT_ADV && BT_HCI_ESP_HOSTED_MCU && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_BT_SILABS_EFR32_LINK_LAYER_STACK_SIZE`: defaults ``1024 if BT_SILABS_EFR32 && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS`` -> ``2048 if SOC_FAMILY_SILABS_S3 && BT_SILABS_EFR32 && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS``, ``1024 if BT_SILABS_EFR32 && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_BT_TX_PROCESSOR_STACK_SIZE`: defaults ``1024 if BT_TX_PROCESSOR_THREAD && BT_HCI && BT`` -> ``2048 if BT_HCI_ESP_HOSTED_MCU && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_ESP32_BT_LE_MAX_EXT_ADV_INSTANCES`: defaults ``1 if ESP32_BT_LE_EXT_ADV && (SOC_SERIES_ESP32C2 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C5 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C6 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32H2) && BT_ESP32 && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS`` -> ``BT_EXT_ADV_MAX_ADV_SET if BT_EXT_ADV && ESP32_BT_LE_EXT_ADV && (SOC_SERIES_ESP32C2 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C5 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C61 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C6 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32H2) && BT_ESP32 && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS``, ``4 if BT_HCI_RAW && ESP32_BT_LE_EXT_ADV && (SOC_SERIES_ESP32C2 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C5 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C61 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C6 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32H2) && BT_ESP32 && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS``, ``1 if ESP32_BT_LE_EXT_ADV && (SOC_SERIES_ESP32C2 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C5 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C61 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C6 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32H2) && BT_ESP32 && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_ESP32_BT_LE_MAX_PERIODIC_SYNCS`: defaults ``1 if ESP32_BT_LE_ENABLE_PERIODIC_ADV && ESP32_BT_LE_50_FEATURE_SUPPORT && (SOC_SERIES_ESP32C2 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C5 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C6 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32H2) && BT_ESP32 && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS``, ``0 if ESP32_BT_LE_50_FEATURE_SUPPORT && (SOC_SERIES_ESP32C2 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C5 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C6 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32H2) && BT_ESP32 && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS`` -> ``BT_PER_ADV_SYNC_MAX if BT_PER_ADV_SYNC && ESP32_BT_LE_50_FEATURE_SUPPORT && (SOC_SERIES_ESP32C2 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C5 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C61 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C6 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32H2) && BT_ESP32 && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS``, ``3 if BT_HCI_RAW && SOC_SERIES_ESP32C2 && ESP32_BT_LE_50_FEATURE_SUPPORT && (SOC_SERIES_ESP32C2 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C5 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C61 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C6 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32H2) && BT_ESP32 && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS``, ``8 if BT_HCI_RAW && ESP32_BT_LE_50_FEATURE_SUPPORT && (SOC_SERIES_ESP32C2 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C5 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C61 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C6 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32H2) && BT_ESP32 && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS``, ``1 if ESP32_BT_LE_ENABLE_PERIODIC_ADV && ESP32_BT_LE_50_FEATURE_SUPPORT && (SOC_SERIES_ESP32C2 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C5 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C61 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C6 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32H2) && BT_ESP32 && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS``, ``0 if ESP32_BT_LE_50_FEATURE_SUPPORT && (SOC_SERIES_ESP32C2 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C5 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C61 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C6 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32H2) && BT_ESP32 && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_ESP32_BT_LE_POWER_CONTROL_ENABLED`: defaults none -> ``y if BT_CTLR_LE_POWER_CONTROL && (SOC_SERIES_ESP32C5 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C61 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C6 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32H2) && ESP32_BT_LE_50_FEATURE_SUPPORT && (SOC_SERIES_ESP32C2 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C5 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C61 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C6 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32H2) && BT_ESP32 && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS``
  * :kconfig:option:`CONFIG_ESP32_BT_LE_SLEEP_ENABLE`: defaults none -> ``y if PM && TICKLESS_KERNEL && (SOC_SERIES_ESP32C2 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C5 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C61 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32C6 || SOC_SERIES_ESP32H2) && BT_ESP32 && BT_HCI && BT_DRIVERS``
